def Tiling.twiceSignedArea : (a b c : Tiling.Plane) → Real :=
fun (a b c : Tiling.Plane) =>
  @HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} Real Real Real (@instHSub.{0} Real Real.instSub)
    (@HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} Real Real Real (@instHMul.{0} Real Real.instMul)
      (@HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} Real Real Real (@instHSub.{0} Real Real.instSub) (Complex.re b) (Complex.re a))
      (@HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} Real Real Real (@instHSub.{0} Real Real.instSub) (Complex.im c) (Complex.im a)))
    (@HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} Real Real Real (@instHMul.{0} Real Real.instMul)
      (@HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} Real Real Real (@instHSub.{0} Real Real.instSub) (Complex.im b) (Complex.im a))
      (@HSub.hSub.{0, 0, 0} Real Real Real (@instHSub.{0} Real Real.instSub) (Complex.re c) (Complex.re a)))
def Tiling.IsTriangle : (s : Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) → Prop :=
fun (s : Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) =>
  @Exists.{1} Tiling.Plane fun (a : Tiling.Plane) =>
    @Exists.{1} Tiling.Plane fun (b : Tiling.Plane) =>
      @Exists.{1} Tiling.Plane fun (c : Tiling.Plane) =>
        And
          (@Ne.{1} Real (Tiling.twiceSignedArea a b c)
            (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0.{0} Real Real.instZero)))
          (@Eq.{1} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) s
            (@DFunLike.coe.{1, 1, 1}
              (@ClosureOperator.{0} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
                (@PartialOrder.toPreorder.{0} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
                  (@ChainCompletePartialOrder.toPartialOrder.{0} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
                    (@ChainCompletePartialOrder.instOfCompleteLattice.{0} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
                      (@CompleteBooleanAlgebra.toCompleteLattice.{0} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
                        (@CompleteAtomicBooleanAlgebra.toCompleteBooleanAlgebra.{0} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
                          (@Set.instCompleteAtomicBooleanAlgebra.{0} Tiling.Plane)))))))
              (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) (fun (x : Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) => Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
              (@ClosureOperator.instFunLike.{0} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
                (@PartialOrder.toPreorder.{0} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
                  (@ChainCompletePartialOrder.toPartialOrder.{0} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
                    (@ChainCompletePartialOrder.instOfCompleteLattice.{0} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
                      (@CompleteBooleanAlgebra.toCompleteLattice.{0} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
                        (@CompleteAtomicBooleanAlgebra.toCompleteBooleanAlgebra.{0} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
                          (@Set.instCompleteAtomicBooleanAlgebra.{0} Tiling.Plane)))))))
              (@convexHull.{0, 0} Real Tiling.Plane Real.semiring Real.partialOrder Complex.instAddCommMonoid
                (@NormedSpace.toModule.{0, 0} Real Tiling.Plane Real.normedField
                  (@NonUnitalSeminormedRing.toSeminormedAddCommGroup.{0} Tiling.Plane
                    (@NonUnitalSeminormedCommRing.toNonUnitalSeminormedRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                      (@SeminormedCommRing.toNonUnitalSeminormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                        (@NormedCommRing.toSeminormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                          (@NormedField.toNormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane Complex.instNormedField)))))
                  (@InnerProductSpace.toNormedSpace.{0, 0} Real Tiling.Plane Real.instRCLike
                    (@NonUnitalSeminormedRing.toSeminormedAddCommGroup.{0} Tiling.Plane
                      (@NonUnitalSeminormedCommRing.toNonUnitalSeminormedRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                        (@SeminormedCommRing.toNonUnitalSeminormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                          (@NormedCommRing.toSeminormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                            (@NormedField.toNormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane Complex.instNormedField)))))
                    instInnerProductSpaceRealComplex)))
              (@Insert.insert.{0, 0} Tiling.Plane (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) (@Set.instInsert.{0} Tiling.Plane) a
                (@Insert.insert.{0, 0} Tiling.Plane (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) (@Set.instInsert.{0} Tiling.Plane) b
                  (@Singleton.singleton.{0, 0} Tiling.Plane (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
                    (@Set.instSingletonSet.{0} Tiling.Plane) c)))))
def Tiling.IsRectangle : (s : Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) → Prop :=
fun (s : Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) =>
  @Exists.{1} Tiling.Plane fun (a : Tiling.Plane) =>
    @Exists.{1} Tiling.Plane fun (b : Tiling.Plane) =>
      And (@Ne.{1} Real (Complex.re a) (Complex.re b))
        (And (@Ne.{1} Real (Complex.im a) (Complex.im b)) (@Eq.{1} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) s (Complex.Rectangle a b)))

fun (s : Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) => Nonempty.{1} (Tiling.Triangulation s)

def Tiling.vertexSet : (s : Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) → Set.{0} Tiling.Plane :=
fun (s : Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) =>
  @Set.ofPred.{0} Tiling.Plane fun (x : Tiling.Plane) =>
    ∀ (triangulation : Tiling.Triangulation s),
      @Exists.{1} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) fun (t : Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) =>
        And
          (@Membership.mem.{0, 0} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) (Finset.{0} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane))
            (@SetLike.instMembership.{0, 0} (Finset.{0} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)) (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
              (@Finset.instSetLike.{0} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)))
            (@Tiling.Triangulation.pieces s triangulation) t)
          (@Membership.mem.{0, 0} Tiling.Plane (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) (@Set.instMembership.{0} Tiling.Plane)
            (@Set.extremePoints.{0, 0} Real Tiling.Plane Real.semiring Real.partialOrder Complex.instAddCommMonoid
              (@Algebra.toSMul.{0, 0} Real Tiling.Plane
                (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{0} Real
                  (@Field.toSemifield.{0} Real (@NormedField.toField.{0} Real Real.normedField)))
                (@Ring.toSemiring.{0} Tiling.Plane
                  (@SeminormedRing.toRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                    (@SeminormedCommRing.toSeminormedRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                      (@NormedCommRing.toSeminormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                        (@NormedField.toNormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane Complex.instNormedField)))))
                (@NormedAlgebra.toAlgebra.{0, 0} Real Tiling.Plane Real.normedField
                  (@SeminormedCommRing.toSeminormedRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                    (@NormedCommRing.toSeminormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                      (@NormedField.toNormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane Complex.instNormedField)))
                  (@RCLike.toNormedAlgebra.{0} Tiling.Plane Complex.instRCLike)))
              t)
            x)
def Tiling.IsRationalPoint : (z : Tiling.Plane) → Prop :=
fun (z : Tiling.Plane) =>
  @Exists.{1} Rat fun (x : Rat) =>
    @Exists.{1} Rat fun (y : Rat) =>
      And (@Eq.{1} Real (Complex.re z) (@Rat.cast.{0} Real Real.instRatCast x))
        (@Eq.{1} Real (Complex.im z) (@Rat.cast.{0} Real Real.instRatCast y))
def Tiling.IsRational : (s : Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) → Prop :=
fun (s : Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) =>
  And (Tiling.IsPolygonal s)
    (∀ (x : Tiling.Plane),
      @Membership.mem.{0, 0} Tiling.Plane (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) (@Set.instMembership.{0} Tiling.Plane)
          (Tiling.vertexSet s) x →
        Tiling.IsRationalPoint x)

  (T : Tiling.Triangulation s) →
    (a : Tiling.Plane) →
      Tiling.Triangulation
        (@HVAdd.hVAdd.{0, 0, 0} Tiling.Plane (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
          (@instHVAdd.{0, 0} Tiling.Plane (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
            (@Set.vaddSet.{0, 0} Tiling.Plane Tiling.Plane (@instVAddOfAdd.{0} Tiling.Plane Complex.instAdd)))
          a s) :=
fun {s : Set.{0} Tiling.Plane} (T : Tiling.Triangulation s) (a : Tiling.Plane) =>
  @Tiling.Triangulation.mk
    (@HVAdd.hVAdd.{0, 0, 0} Tiling.Plane (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
      (@instHVAdd.{0, 0} Tiling.Plane (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
        (@Set.vaddSet.{0, 0} Tiling.Plane Tiling.Plane (@instVAddOfAdd.{0} Tiling.Plane Complex.instAdd)))
      a s)
    (@Finset.image.{0, 0} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) (@Set.decidableEq.{0} Tiling.Plane)
      (fun (x : Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) =>
        @HVAdd.hVAdd.{0, 0, 0} Tiling.Plane (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)

            (@Set.vaddSet.{0, 0} Tiling.Plane Tiling.Plane (@instVAddOfAdd.{0} Tiling.Plane Complex.instAdd)))
          a x)

    (@Tiling.Triangulation.vadd._proof_3 s T a)

      Tiling.IsPolygonal

          (@Tiling.PolygonalProtoTiling.prototiles.{u} P self p))

def Tiling.PolygonalProtoTiling.shiftedPlacement.{u} : {P : Type u} →
  (A : Tiling.PolygonalProtoTiling.{u} P) →
    (v : Tiling.Plane) →
      (shift : @Tiling.PolygonalProtoTiling.Plate.{u} P A → Real) →
        (i : @Set.Elem.{u} (Tiling.Placement.{0, u} Tiling.Plane P) (@Tiling.PolygonalProtoTiling.placements.{u} P A)) →
          Tiling.Placement.{0, u} Tiling.Plane P :=
fun {P : Type u} (A : Tiling.PolygonalProtoTiling.{u} P) (v : Tiling.Plane)
    (shift : @Tiling.PolygonalProtoTiling.Plate.{u} P A → Real)
    (i : @Set.Elem.{u} (Tiling.Placement.{0, u} Tiling.Plane P) (@Tiling.PolygonalProtoTiling.placements.{u} P A)) =>
  @Prod.mk.{0, u} Tiling.Plane P
    (@HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} Tiling.Plane Tiling.Plane Tiling.Plane (@instHAdd.{0} Tiling.Plane Complex.instAdd)
      (@Prod.fst.{0, u} Tiling.Plane P
        (@Subtype.val.{u + 1} (Tiling.Placement.{0, u} Tiling.Plane P)
          (fun (x : Tiling.Placement.{0, u} Tiling.Plane P) =>
            @Membership.mem.{u, u} (Tiling.Placement.{0, u} Tiling.Plane P)
              (Set.{u} (Tiling.Placement.{0, u} Tiling.Plane P))
              (@Set.instMembership.{u} (Tiling.Placement.{0, u} Tiling.Plane P))
              (@Tiling.PolygonalProtoTiling.placements.{u} P A) x)
          i))
      (@HSMul.hSMul.{0, 0, 0} Real Tiling.Plane Tiling.Plane
        (@instHSMul.{0, 0} Real Tiling.Plane
          (@Algebra.toSMul.{0, 0} Real Tiling.Plane
            (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{0} Real
              (@Field.toSemifield.{0} Real (@NormedField.toField.{0} Real Real.normedField)))
            (@Ring.toSemiring.{0} Tiling.Plane
              (@SeminormedRing.toRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                (@SeminormedCommRing.toSeminormedRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                  (@NormedCommRing.toSeminormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                    (@NormedField.toNormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane Complex.instNormedField)))))
            (@NormedAlgebra.toAlgebra.{0, 0} Real Tiling.Plane Real.normedField
              (@SeminormedCommRing.toSeminormedRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                (@NormedCommRing.toSeminormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                  (@NormedField.toNormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane Complex.instNormedField)))
              (@RCLike.toNormedAlgebra.{0} Tiling.Plane Complex.instRCLike))))
        (shift
          (@SimpleGraph.connectedComponentMk.{u}
            (@Set.Elem.{u} (Tiling.Placement.{0, u} Tiling.Plane P) (@Tiling.PolygonalProtoTiling.placements.{u} P A))
            (@Tiling.PolygonalProtoTiling.vertexGraph.{u} P A) i))
        v))
    (@Prod.snd.{0, u} Tiling.Plane P
      (@Subtype.val.{u + 1} (Tiling.Placement.{0, u} Tiling.Plane P)
        (fun (x : Tiling.Placement.{0, u} Tiling.Plane P) =>
          @Membership.mem.{u, u} (Tiling.Placement.{0, u} Tiling.Plane P)
            (Set.{u} (Tiling.Placement.{0, u} Tiling.Plane P))
            (@Set.instMembership.{u} (Tiling.Placement.{0, u} Tiling.Plane P))
            (@Tiling.PolygonalProtoTiling.placements.{u} P A) x)
        i))

def Tiling.PolygonalProtoTiling.shiftedVertex.{u} : {P : Type u} →
  (A : Tiling.PolygonalProtoTiling.{u} P) →
    (v : Tiling.Plane) →
      (shift : @Tiling.PolygonalProtoTiling.Plate.{u} P A → Real) →
        (i : @Set.Elem.{u} (Tiling.Placement.{0, u} Tiling.Plane P) (@Tiling.PolygonalProtoTiling.placements.{u} P A)) →

fun {P : Type u} (A : Tiling.PolygonalProtoTiling.{u} P) (v : Tiling.Plane)
    (shift : @Tiling.PolygonalProtoTiling.Plate.{u} P A → Real)
    (i : @Set.Elem.{u} (Tiling.Placement.{0, u} Tiling.Plane P) (@Tiling.PolygonalProtoTiling.placements.{u} P A)) =>
  @Subtype.mk.{u + 1} (Tiling.Placement.{0, u} Tiling.Plane P)
    (fun (x : Tiling.Placement.{0, u} Tiling.Plane P) =>
      @Membership.mem.{u, u} (Tiling.Placement.{0, u} Tiling.Plane P) (Set.{u} (Tiling.Placement.{0, u} Tiling.Plane P))
        (@Set.instMembership.{u} (Tiling.Placement.{0, u} Tiling.Plane P))

          (@ChainCompletePartialOrder.instOfCompleteLattice.{0} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
            (@CompleteBooleanAlgebra.toCompleteLattice.{0} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
              (@CompleteAtomicBooleanAlgebra.toCompleteBooleanAlgebra.{0} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
                (@Set.instCompleteAtomicBooleanAlgebra.{0} Tiling.Plane)))))
        (@HeytingAlgebra.toOrderBot.{0} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
          (@Order.Frame.toHeytingAlgebra.{0} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
            (@CompleteDistribLattice.toFrame.{0} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
              (@CompleteBooleanAlgebra.toCompleteDistribLattice.{0} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
                (@CompleteAtomicBooleanAlgebra.toCompleteBooleanAlgebra.{0} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
                  (@Set.instCompleteAtomicBooleanAlgebra.{0} Tiling.Plane))))))
        (@Set.univ.{0} (Fin numberOfParts)) part)
    (iUnion_parts :
      @Eq.{1} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane)
        (@Set.iUnion.{0, 1} Tiling.Plane (Fin numberOfParts) fun (i : Fin numberOfParts) => part i) A) :
    Tiling.RationalPolygonal.WeakPeriodicDecomposition A
def Tiling.RationalPolygonal.WeaklyPeriodic : (A : Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) → Prop :=

def Tiling.RationalPolygonal.DoublyPeriodic : (A : Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) → Prop :=
fun (A : Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) =>
  @Exists.{1} Tiling.Plane fun (h₁ : Tiling.Plane) =>
    @Exists.{1} Tiling.Plane fun (h₂ : Tiling.Plane) =>
      And
        (@LinearIndependent.{0, 0, 0}
          (Fin (Nat.succ (Nat.succ (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 0) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 0)))))) Real Tiling.Plane
          (@Matrix.vecCons.{0} Tiling.Plane (Nat.succ (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 0) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 0)))) h₁
            (@Matrix.vecCons.{0} Tiling.Plane (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 0) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 0))) h₂
              (@Matrix.vecEmpty.{0} Tiling.Plane)))
          Real.semiring Complex.instAddCommMonoid
          (@NormedSpace.toModule.{0, 0} Real Tiling.Plane Real.normedField
            (@NonUnitalSeminormedRing.toSeminormedAddCommGroup.{0} Tiling.Plane
              (@NonUnitalSeminormedCommRing.toNonUnitalSeminormedRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                (@SeminormedCommRing.toNonUnitalSeminormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                  (@NormedCommRing.toSeminormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                    (@NormedField.toNormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane Complex.instNormedField)))))
            (@InnerProductSpace.toNormedSpace.{0, 0} Real Tiling.Plane Real.instRCLike
              (@NonUnitalSeminormedRing.toSeminormedAddCommGroup.{0} Tiling.Plane
                (@NonUnitalSeminormedCommRing.toNonUnitalSeminormedRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                  (@SeminormedCommRing.toNonUnitalSeminormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                    (@NormedCommRing.toSeminormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                      (@NormedField.toNormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane Complex.instNormedField)))))
              instInnerProductSpaceRealComplex)))
        (And (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.SetPeriodicBy A h₁) (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.SetPeriodicBy A h₂))

          Tiling.IsRationalPoint

              v))

      Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RationalPolygonalRegion.denormalizedTranslations K
        (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.WeakPeriodicDecomposition.part A D i))
    (fun (i : Fin (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.WeakPeriodicDecomposition.numberOfParts A D)) =>
      @HSMul.hSMul.{0, 0, 0} Real Tiling.Plane Tiling.Plane
        (@instHSMul.{0, 0} Real Tiling.Plane
          (@Algebra.toSMul.{0, 0} Real Tiling.Plane
            (@Semifield.toCommSemiring.{0} Real
              (@Field.toSemifield.{0} Real (@NormedField.toField.{0} Real Real.normedField)))

                (@SeminormedCommRing.toSeminormedRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                  (@NormedCommRing.toSeminormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                    (@NormedField.toNormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane Complex.instNormedField)))))
            (@NormedAlgebra.toAlgebra.{0, 0} Real Tiling.Plane Real.normedField
              (@SeminormedCommRing.toSeminormedRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                (@NormedCommRing.toSeminormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane
                  (@NormedField.toNormedCommRing.{0} Tiling.Plane Complex.instNormedField)))
              (@RCLike.toNormedAlgebra.{0} Tiling.Plane Complex.instRCLike))))

def Tiling.RationalPolygonal.discreteCarrier.{u} : {G : Type u} →
  [AddCommGroup.{u} G] → (F : Finset.{u} G) → (T : Set.{u} G) → Set.{u} G :=
fun {G : Type u} [inst : AddCommGroup.{u} G] (F : Finset.{u} G) (T : Set.{u} G) =>
  @Set.ofPred.{u} G fun (x : G) =>
    @Exists.{u + 1} G fun (f : G) =>
      And
        (@Membership.mem.{u, u} G (Finset.{u} G)
          (@SetLike.instMembership.{u, u} (Finset.{u} G) G (@Finset.instSetLike.{u} G)) F f)
        (@Exists.{u + 1} G fun (t : G) =>
          And (@Membership.mem.{u, u} G (Set.{u} G) (@Set.instMembership.{u} G) T t)
            (@Eq.{u + 1} G x
              (@HAdd.hAdd.{u, u, u} G G G
                (@instHAdd.{u} G
                  (@AddCommMagma.toAdd.{u} G
                    (@AddCommSemigroup.toAddCommMagma.{u} G
                      (@AddCommMonoid.toAddCommSemigroup.{u} G (@AddCommGroup.toAddCommMonoid.{u} G inst)))))
                f t)))
def Tiling.RationalPolygonal.shiftFinset.{u} : {G : Type u} →
  [AddCommGroup.{u} G] → (q : G) → (F : Finset.{u} G) → Finset.{u} G :=
fun {G : Type u} [inst : AddCommGroup.{u} G] (q : G) (F : Finset.{u} G) =>
  @Finset.image.{u, u} G G (fun (a b : G) => Classical.propDecidable (@Eq.{u + 1} G a b))
    (fun (f : G) =>
      @HSub.hSub.{u, u, u} G G G
        (@instHSub.{u} G
          (@SubNegMonoid.toSub.{u} G (@AddGroup.toSubNegMonoid.{u} G (@AddCommGroup.toAddGroup.{u} G inst))))
        f q)
    F
def Tiling.RationalPolygonal.DiscretePeriodicBy.{u} : {G : Type u} →
  [AddCommGroup.{u} G] → (A : Set.{u} G) → (v : G) → Prop :=
fun {G : Type u} [inst : AddCommGroup.{u} G] (A : Set.{u} G) (v : G) =>
  ∀ (x : G),
    Iff
      (@Membership.mem.{u, u} G (Set.{u} G) (@Set.instMembership.{u} G) A
        (@HAdd.hAdd.{u, u, u} G G G
          (@instHAdd.{u} G
            (@AddCommMagma.toAdd.{u} G
              (@AddCommSemigroup.toAddCommMagma.{u} G
                (@AddCommMonoid.toAddCommSemigroup.{u} G (@AddCommGroup.toAddCommMonoid.{u} G inst)))))
          x v))
      (@Membership.mem.{u, u} G (Set.{u} G) (@Set.instMembership.{u} G) A x)
def Tiling.RationalPolygonal.quakeTransfer.{u} : {G : Type u} →
  [AddCommGroup.{u} G] → (F : Finset.{u} G) → (T : Set.{u} G) → (v : G) → (t t' : @Set.Elem.{u} G T) → Prop :=
fun {G : Type u} [inst : AddCommGroup.{u} G] (F : Finset.{u} G) (T : Set.{u} G) (v : G) (t t' : @Set.Elem.{u} G T) =>
  @Exists.{u + 1} G fun (f : G) =>
    And
      (@Membership.mem.{u, u} G (Finset.{u} G)
        (@SetLike.instMembership.{u, u} (Finset.{u} G) G (@Finset.instSetLike.{u} G)) F f)
      (@Exists.{u + 1} G fun (f' : G) =>
        And
          (@Membership.mem.{u, u} G (Finset.{u} G)
            (@SetLike.instMembership.{u, u} (Finset.{u} G) G (@Finset.instSetLike.{u} G)) F f')
          (@Eq.{u + 1} G
            (@HAdd.hAdd.{u, u, u} G G G
              (@instHAdd.{u} G
                (@AddCommMagma.toAdd.{u} G
                  (@AddCommSemigroup.toAddCommMagma.{u} G
                    (@AddCommMonoid.toAddCommSemigroup.{u} G (@AddCommGroup.toAddCommMonoid.{u} G inst)))))
              (@HAdd.hAdd.{u, u, u} G G G
                (@instHAdd.{u} G
                  (@AddCommMagma.toAdd.{u} G
                    (@AddCommSemigroup.toAddCommMagma.{u} G
                      (@AddCommMonoid.toAddCommSemigroup.{u} G (@AddCommGroup.toAddCommMonoid.{u} G inst)))))
                f
                (@Subtype.val.{u + 1} G
                  (fun (x : G) => @Membership.mem.{u, u} G (Set.{u} G) (@Set.instMembership.{u} G) T x) t))
              v)
            (@HAdd.hAdd.{u, u, u} G G G
              (@instHAdd.{u} G
                (@AddCommMagma.toAdd.{u} G
                  (@AddCommSemigroup.toAddCommMagma.{u} G

@[reducible] def Tiling.RationalPolygonal.QuakePlate.{u} : {G : Type u} →

  @SimpleGraph.ConnectedComponent.{u} (@Set.Elem.{u} G T) (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.quakeGraph.{u} G inst F T v)
def Tiling.RationalPolygonal.quakePlateTranslations.{u} : {G : Type u} →
  [inst : AddCommGroup.{u} G] →
    {F : Finset.{u} G} →
      {T : Set.{u} G} → {v : G} → (C : @Tiling.RationalPolygonal.QuakePlate.{u} G inst F T v) → Set.{u} G :=

    (C : @Tiling.RationalPolygonal.QuakePlate.{u} G inst F T v) =>
  @Set.ofPred.{u} G fun (t : G) =>
    @Exists.{0} (@Membership.mem.{u, u} G (Set.{u} G) (@Set.instMembership.{u} G) T t)
      fun (ht : @Membership.mem.{u, u} G (Set.{u} G) (@Set.instMembership.{u} G) T t) =>
      @Eq.{u + 1}
        (@SimpleGraph.ConnectedComponent.{u} (@Set.Elem.{u} G T)
          (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.quakeGraph.{u} G inst F T v))

def Tiling.RationalPolygonal.quakeShiftedTranslations.{u} : {G : Type u} →
  [inst : AddCommGroup.{u} G] →
    {F : Finset.{u} G} →
      {T : Set.{u} G} → {v : G} → (shift : @Tiling.RationalPolygonal.QuakePlate.{u} G inst F T v → Int) → Set.{u} G :=

def Tiling.RationalPolygonal.quakeIncompleteOrbit.{u} : {G : Type u} →
  [inst : AddCommGroup.{u} G] →
    {F : Finset.{u} G} →
      {T : Set.{u} G} →
        {v : G} →
          (C : @Tiling.RationalPolygonal.QuakePlate.{u} G inst F T v) →
            (Λ : @AddSubgroup.{u} G (@AddCommGroup.toAddGroup.{u} G inst)) →
              (c :
                  @HasQuotient.Quotient.{u, u} G (@AddSubgroup.{u} G (@AddCommGroup.toAddGroup.{u} G inst))
                    (@QuotientAddGroup.instHasQuotientAddSubgroup.{u} G (@AddCommGroup.toAddGroup.{u} G inst)) Λ) →
                (p : G) → Set.{u} G :=
fun {G : Type u} [inst : AddCommGroup.{u} G] {F : Finset.{u} G} {T : Set.{u} G} {v : G}
    (C : @Tiling.RationalPolygonal.QuakePlate.{u} G inst F T v)
    (Λ : @AddSubgroup.{u} G (@AddCommGroup.toAddGroup.{u} G inst))
    (c :
      @HasQuotient.Quotient.{u, u} G (@AddSubgroup.{u} G (@AddCommGroup.toAddGroup.{u} G inst))
        (@QuotientAddGroup.instHasQuotientAddSubgroup.{u} G (@AddCommGroup.toAddGroup.{u} G inst)) Λ)
    (p : G) =>
  @Set.ofPred.{u} G fun (x : G) =>
    And
      (@Membership.mem.{u, u} G (Set.{u} G) (@Set.instMembership.{u} G)
        (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.quakePlateTranslations.{u} G inst F T v C) x)
      (And
        (@Membership.mem.{u, u} G (Set.{u} G) (@Set.instMembership.{u} G)
          (@TranslationalTilings.quotientCoset.{u} G inst Λ c) x)
        (@Exists.{1} Int fun (n : Int) =>
          Not
            (@Membership.mem.{u, u} G (Set.{u} G) (@Set.instMembership.{u} G)
              (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.quakePlateTranslations.{u} G inst F T v C)
              (@HAdd.hAdd.{u, u, u} G G G
                (@instHAdd.{u} G
                  (@AddCommMagma.toAdd.{u} G
                    (@AddCommSemigroup.toAddCommMagma.{u} G
                      (@AddCommMonoid.toAddCommSemigroup.{u} G (@AddCommGroup.toAddCommMonoid.{u} G inst)))))
                x
                (@HSMul.hSMul.{0, u, u} Int G G
                  (@instHSMul.{0, u} Int G
                    (@ZSMul.toSMul.{u} G
                      (@SubNegMonoid.toZSMul.{u} G
                        (@AddGroup.toSubNegMonoid.{u} G (@AddCommGroup.toAddGroup.{u} G inst)))))
                  n p)))))

        (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.signature K u)
        (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.translations K T R)
        (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.direction K T R) D C j))
def Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.assembledQuakePart : {K :
    Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion} →
  {T : Set.{0} Tiling.Plane} →

      {u : Tiling.Plane} →

            Tiling.RationalPolygonal.UniformQuakePeriodicDecomposition

          (j :
              Fin
                (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.UniformQuakePeriodicDecomposition.numberOfParts
                  (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.signature K u)

                  (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.direction K T R) D)) →
            Set.{0} Tiling.Plane :=

    (R : Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction K T) {u : Tiling.Plane}
    (D :
      Tiling.RationalPolygonal.UniformQuakePeriodicDecomposition
        (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.signature K u)

        (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.direction K T R))
    (j :
      Fin
        (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.UniformQuakePeriodicDecomposition.numberOfParts
          (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.signature K u)
          (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.translations K T R)
          (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.direction K T R) D)) =>
  @Set.iUnion.{0, 1} Tiling.Plane
    (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.QuakePlate.{0} Tiling.RationalPolygonal.IntegerPlane
      (@Pi.addCommGroup.{0, 0} (Fin (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2))))
        (fun (a : Fin (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) => Int)
        fun (i : Fin (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) => Int.instAddCommGroup)
      (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.signature K u)
      (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.translations K T R)
      (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.direction K T R))
    fun
      (C :
        @Tiling.RationalPolygonal.QuakePlate.{0} Tiling.RationalPolygonal.IntegerPlane
          (@Pi.addCommGroup.{0, 0} (Fin (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2))))
            (fun (a : Fin (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) => Int)

          (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.signature K u)
          (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.translations K T R)

    @Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.unshiftedQuakePart K T R u D C j
def Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.weakPeriodicDecomposition : {K :
    Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion} →
  {T : Set.{0} Tiling.Plane} →
    (R : Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction K T) →
      {u : Tiling.Plane} →
        (hTiles :
            @TranslationalTilings.TilesUniverse.{0} Tiling.RationalPolygonal.IntegerPlane
              (@Pi.addCommGroup.{0, 0} (Fin (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2))))
                (fun (a : Fin (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) => Int)
                fun (i : Fin (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) => Int.instAddCommGroup)
              (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.signature K u)
              (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.translations K T R)) →
          (D :
              Tiling.RationalPolygonal.UniformQuakePeriodicDecomposition
                (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.signature K u)
                (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.translations K T R)
                (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.direction K T R)) →
            Tiling.RationalPolygonal.WeakPeriodicDecomposition T :=
fun {K : Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion} {T : Set.{0} Tiling.Plane}
    (R : Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction K T) {u : Tiling.Plane}
    (hTiles :
      @TranslationalTilings.TilesUniverse.{0} Tiling.RationalPolygonal.IntegerPlane
        (@Pi.addCommGroup.{0, 0} (Fin (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2))))
          (fun (a : Fin (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) => Int)
          fun (i : Fin (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2)))) => Int.instAddCommGroup)
        (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.signature K u)
        (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.translations K T R))
    (D :
      Tiling.RationalPolygonal.UniformQuakePeriodicDecomposition
        (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.signature K u)
        (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.translations K T R)
        (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.direction K T R)) =>
  @Tiling.RationalPolygonal.WeakPeriodicDecomposition.mk T
    (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.UniformQuakePeriodicDecomposition.numberOfParts
      (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.signature K u)
      (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.translations K T R)
      (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.direction K T R) D)
    (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.assembledQuakePart K T R u D)
    (fun
        (j :
          Fin
            (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.UniformQuakePeriodicDecomposition.numberOfParts
              (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.signature K u)
              (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.translations K T R)
              (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.direction K T R) D)) =>
      Tiling.RationalPolygonal.integerPoint
        (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.UniformQuakePeriodicDecomposition.period
          (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.signature K u)
          (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.translations K T R)
          (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.direction K T R) D j))
    (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.weakPeriodicDecomposition._proof_1 K T R u D)
    (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.assembledQuakePart_periodic K T R u D)
    (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.assembledQuakePart_pairwiseDisjoint K T R u
      hTiles D)
    (@Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.IntegerSlidingReduction.iUnion_assembledQuakePart K T R u hTiles D)
structure Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation : Type
number of parameters: 0
fields:
  Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation.rasterTile : Finset.{0} Tiling.RationalPolygonal.IntegerPlane
constructor:
  Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation.mk (rasterTile : Finset.{0} Tiling.RationalPolygonal.IntegerPlane) :
    Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation
def Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation.Represents : (P : Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation) →
  (K : Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RationalPolygonalRegion) → Prop :=
fun (P : Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation) (K : Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RationalPolygonalRegion) =>
  @Eq.{1} (Finset.{0} Tiling.RationalPolygonal.IntegerPlane) (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation.rasterTile P)
    (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.relevantPlanarRasterTile
      (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RationalPolygonalRegion.normalizedRegion K))
def Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation.tileDecision : (P : Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation) →
  Bool :=
fun (P : Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation) =>
  TranslationalTilings.planarTileDecision (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation.rasterTile P)
def Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation.tileDecisionCost : (P : Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation) →
  Nat :=
fun (P : Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation) =>
  TranslationalTilings.planarTileSearchCost (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation.rasterTile P)
def Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation.DecidesTileability : (algorithm :
    Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation → Bool) →
  Prop :=
fun (algorithm : Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation → Bool) =>
  ∀ (P : Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation) (K : Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RationalPolygonalRegion),
    Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation.Represents P K →
      Iff (@Eq.{1} Bool (algorithm P) Bool.true)
        (@Exists.{1} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) fun (T : Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) =>
          Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.Tiles
            (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RationalPolygonalRegion.toPolygonalRegion K) T)
def Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation.tileableDecidable : (P : Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation) →
  (K : Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RationalPolygonalRegion) →
    (hP : Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation.Represents P K) →
      Decidable
        (@Exists.{1} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) fun (T : Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) =>
          Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.Tiles
            (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RationalPolygonalRegion.toPolygonalRegion K) T) :=
fun (P : Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation) (K : Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RationalPolygonalRegion)
    (hP : Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation.Represents P K) =>
  @decidable_of_iff
    (@Exists.{1} (Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) fun (T : Set.{0} Tiling.Plane) =>
      Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.Tiles
        (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RationalPolygonalRegion.toPolygonalRegion K) T)
    (@Eq.{1} Bool (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation.tileDecision P) Bool.true)
    (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation.tileDecision_eq_true_iff P K hP)
    (instDecidableEqBool (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation.tileDecision P) Bool.true)
def Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation.canonical : (K : Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RationalPolygonalRegion) →
  Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation :=
fun (K : Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RationalPolygonalRegion) =>
  Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RasterPresentation.mk
    (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.PolygonalRegion.relevantPlanarRasterTile
      (Tiling.RationalPolygonal.RationalPolygonalRegion.normalizedRegion K))
